Wire nine baselined workspace/file operations through existing Client/DaemonConnection: change/validate directory, check/grant folder trust, list/search/read files, daemon-side URL push/pull. Newer write-only schema is not a required baselined SDK helper.

** Notes

Nine baselined workspace/file Client and DaemonConnection operations implemented: check/grant folder trust, validate/change directory, list/search/read files, daemon-side URL push/pull. Existing Workspace/Control codecs unchanged. Low-level list/search materialize hidden=false and search60; high-level search uses50 unless explicit. Standard connection adapter generalized by deadline: ordinary30s, transfers900000000us (15m), matching TypeScript expiry constant8118. Low-level caller CallOptions remain unchanged. Shared notification method parameter supports setup progress and existing archive observation without new readers/workers or lifetime paths.

Paths and URLs remain explicit daemon-side data. No local path resolution/cwd change/filesystem transfer, implicit trust grant/session load/state-store update or remote cancellation/rollback claim. Upstream workspace-name transfer aliases are the same session-scoped wire capability. The supplied newer write-file codec remains untouched; no absent baselined helper was invented for schema coverage.

Six native cases verify exact authenticated requests/defaults/trust flags/read metadata/unchanged cwd; low-level60 and zero-deadline no-send; actual binary PUT/GET and written/read bytes in a mock daemon directory via loopback HTTP with no SDK-side destination; setup/malformed events; remote/malformed results; cancelled transfer wait preserving AsyncCancelled/reuse and issuing no cancel RPC. Existing codec tests retain field/null/extension/count coverage. First compilation found redundant test import/name shadowing; fixed, not suppressed.

Fresh build all-Werror and3144 tests pass(8.07s), focused Ormolu/HLint and five reference audits pass. Exact README WorkspaceExample compiles-fno-code-Werror without execution. No source SDK edits, real Factory/daemon/trust/model/OAuth calls, commits or repeated package cycles.

Fess self-audit completed before closure: no production stubs, suppressed warnings, availability-based fallback, new duplicate runtime or unrelated refactor. The external mock daemon/HTTP fixture exercises actual bytes and explicitly asserts results, not only method invocation. Wire fixtures derive from baselined SDK source and existing schemas. No new catch/swallow in client operations; observer isolation and async behavior remain the existing dispatcher contract. Workspace URLs/content stay sensitive explicit fields with redacted record displays. Verification gaps:15m deadline source/config checked, not elapsed-wallclock tested; no independent review/live/other-platform/package sign-off this slice. No scope reduction: terminals/worktrees/default settings/queues/stores/advanced resources and full hosted validation/licensing remain open. Next:terminal and remaining resource APIs.

Implement the baseline daemon text-object framing over a native, owned plain WebSocket connection. Use websockets protocol machinery and bracket socket lifetime so failed/cancelled connect/handshake and bounded close cannot leak resources. Cover text/UTF-8/binary rejection, sizes, close/EOF, handshake failure/deadline, cancellation and RPC integration with local peers. TLS, daemon/relay authentication, reconnect/retries and binary tunnels remain explicit later layers. No Factory calls, tokens or live mutations.

** Notes

The prior local-MVP deferral is superseded by the user-directed daemon delivery. Factory.Droid.Transport.WebSocket is packaged and verified with offline peers: owned socket lifetime across setup/cleanup, explicit plain or verified TLS, trusted/private-CA and hostname behavior, bounded header/frame/message/connect/close paths, JSON/UTF-8/binary validation, cancellation identity and scoped RPC integration. TLS and daemon authentication were delivered together under hsdk-transports-client-4yp rather than left as insecure follow-ups. The archived prototype remains separate historical material. Evidence: /tmp/droid-daemon-milestone.nd8zgR. Relay/reconnect/binary tunnels remain separate functional work; no live call was made.

Implement validated image and text/PDF attachment values and high-level submission through the existing AddUserMessageParams turn path. Follow Python _high_level/attachments.py: 5 MiB images/text, 3 MiB PDF, strict base64 and signatures, UTF-8 byte limits, metadata preservation, bounded regular-file reads and change detection. Use asynchronous-exception-safe FD/handle ownership and prevent FIFO replacement from hanging an open. Reconcile aggregate attachments with the existing 10 MiB high-level JSONL frame ceiling without silent truncation or unsafe constructors. Test all required source forms, invalid inputs, file/race boundaries, exact wire payloads and resource cleanup offline. No live calls or unused codec expansion.

** Notes

Implemented Factory.Droid.Input with private validated image/text/PDF values, source/byte/file constructors, metadata preservation, redacted errors and bounded regular-file IO. Added four sendDroidInput variants through existing AddUserMessageParams/Client/turn ownership. Frame limits remain 10 MiB by default but are configurable; native tests prove 2x5 MiB images are rejected at default and accepted at 20 MiB without truncation. Canonical Base64 pad-bit rejection is intentionally stricter than Python aliases and documented. Review found no implementation defect; its FD/race coverage gap was addressed by a tracked GHCi actual-source breakpoint probe and CI wiring. Probes cover FIFO/inode replacement, observed mutation, ThreadKilled plus closed FD, forced short-read continuation and exact read budget. Snapshot-check mutant failed then guard restored. Final review found no issues; reviewer inspected source/logs only. Verified 2841 Haskell tests (123 high-level, 34 input), five audit tests, 20x203 parallel checks, -Werror build, HLint/Ormolu/actionlint, compiled attachment example and Haddock after fetching Base64 source for docs. Existing link/no-repository warnings remain. Package file list includes new code/tests/probe and optional backlog document. Evidence: /tmp/droid-attachments.5GtLNM. No new live Factory/model call, full sdist rebuild or new platform execution.

Implement fork, compaction and rewind through one local replacement path: reject active turns, drain existing ordinary requests, load successor on the same connection, retire the old handle only after success, restore the original on failed load, and fail closed when rollback/outcome is uncertain. Preserve cancellation identity, scope ownership, permission policy and returned metadata. Add deterministic native lifecycle/concurrency tests; no live/cost-bearing calls without fresh authorization.

** Notes

Implemented forkDroidSession, compactDroidSession and rewindDroidSession via one replacement path. Shared SessionConnection owns RPC/channel/dispatcher/IDs/turn lock/availability; per-handle STM phase and leases prevent new source calls and drain existing calls before switching. Successors share the original scope, source retirement is atomic, load failure attempts original reload, rollback failure and uncertain operation outcomes invalidate. Added DroidSessionStatus, DroidReplacementError and scoped rewind options. Cancellation after the retirement commit can obscure result delivery but not scope ownership; target ID remains recorded. A reviewer withdrew an initial stronger handoff objection after agreeing this explicit IO linearization contract.

Review exposed and native traces reproduced a real cancelled-mutation crossing bug. Read-only/mutating effects are now explicit, mutation outcome classification/invalidation is masked before lease release, and replacement drain/cleanup cannot erase invalidation. A repeated-cancellation regression covers the narrowed critical window. Removing the drain guard made its regression fail; it was restored. Gated peers observe requests independently of session usability, prove writer release before healthy load cancellation, and verify actual rollback attempts. Rollback-cancellation, post-commit cancellation and independent-connection tests are included.

Added synchronizeRpcEvents as an internal queued-prefix boundary over the existing channel intake: no wire frame and no second reader. Startup/load includes event-prefix dispatch in its 60-second budget so prior history cannot enter the next turn. Four dispatcher regressions cover response/notification overtaking, empty-prefix completion, cancellation and terminal wakeup; native load replay covers both successor and original rollback loads.

Verification: all 48 high-level cases, 20 dispatcher cases and all 2732 tests pass; -Werror build, HLint/Ormolu pass; 50 parallel repetitions of the 94 channel/dispatcher/high-level cases pass. README replacement example compiles with -fno-code. Haddock completes with retained existing warnings. Independent final recheck found no issues in the fixes; reviewer executed no tests and inspected parent evidence. Artifacts: /tmp/droid-replacement-check.R1qMK9 (verified-checks.log, verified-stress-*.log, mutation-before.log, drain-mutant.log, working-diff.patch, ReplacementExample.hs, haddock.log). * DONE [#C] Bound local JSONL subprocess shutdown
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       hsdk-amj
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: task

:MODIFIED: [2026-09-06 Sun 08:14]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-09-06 Sun 08:14]
:CLOSE_REASON: Shutdown increment verified: TERM/grace/KILL/reaping, buffered-write cancellation regression, 2384 tests, 30 stress runs and independent source distribution.
:SOURCE_REPO: droid-sdk-haskell
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/work/positron/droid-sdk-haskell
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Completed the approved shutdown increment in src/Factory/Droid/Transport/Process.hs and test/ProcessSpec.hs. withJsonLinesProcess now takes frame-byte limit, Int grace in microseconds, process configuration and callback. Nonpositive grace skips the wait. It sends SIGTERM, uses interruptible nonblocking exit polling during grace, then SIGKILLs only the still-owned child and reaps it with asynchronous exceptions deferred. No competing/cancelled waitForProcess waiter is introduced. Pipes close after termination/reaping; process's existing ignoreSigPipe helper ignores only EPIPE from closing terminated stdin.

Two failures were reproduced: SIGTERM-resistant cleanup waited for the 5-second fixture watchdog (5.02s); repeated small-frame writes left a buffered stdin flush, causing pre-termination hClose to wait for that watchdog and replace AsyncCancelled. NoBuffering alone did not fix the latter. Terminating before closing pipes fixed it. The native peer's watchdog prevents test process leaks but cannot satisfy the separate 2-second cleanup assertion.

The 13 process tests cover request/acknowledgement/deltas/completion, malformed frames, limits, EOF/truncation, cooperative exit, ignored SIGTERM, nonpositive grace/natural-exit races, callback failure, cancellation during receive, small/large full-pipe writes and cancellation during grace. Every peer PID is checked reaped. All 2384 Haskell tests pass with -Werror; the final 13-test group passed 30 consecutive parallel runs (4 test workers, 2 GHC capabilities). Ormolu, HLint, actionlint and 5 audit regressions pass. Haddock covers the module; existing dependency/Generic-link warnings and cabal's no-repository advisory remain. README's new echo example returns True in isolated GHCi.

Independent source distribution: /tmp/droid-shutdown-final.tlkaHJ/droid-sdk-0.1.0.0. The combined verification command hit its 600-second tool timeout during the fresh source build, without a compiler error; completing that build in a separate command passed all 2384 tests and the 5 audit tests. Logs: build.log, build-resume.log, haddock.log and repl.log in the parent artifact directory. Read-only reviewer found no issues in the initial escalation design; the stronger buffered-write regression and fix were found and verified by the coordinator afterward.

Limits remain explicit: no absolute kernel-exit deadline, descendant management, Droid launcher, request dispatcher or live interoperability claim. No new schema family, live Factory call, commit or push. Broader transport/client milestone remains open.

* DONE [#C] Reconcile diagnostic text limits between JSON Schema and Zod
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       hsdk-diagnostic-text-limits-81n
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: bug
:CREATED:  [2026-09-05 Sat 10:40]
:MODIFIED: [2026-09-09 Wed 07:13]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-09-09 Wed 07:13]
:CLOSE_REASON: Resolved wire/runtime length distinction without narrowing canonical BugReportSource/BoundedText codecs. New validateBugReportSource applies baselined CLI/TypeScript UTF-16 limits via standard text encoding and reflected bounds; typed errors contain field/limit, not values. Both local Client.submitBugReport and new Client.submitDaemonBugReport/Daemon.submitBugReport use one preflight before report dispatch. Explicit comment/log/source/session fields and extensions are preserved; no truncation, normalization, inferred attribution, local collection, retry or implicit load. Python baseline has no source field. Extracted TS 0.7.0 schema with Zod 3.24.0 matches 45 Unicode vectors/five null rejections; pinned CLI static source near byte204339270 has the same bounds. A native 51-emoji local request failed before the guard and now rejects before dispatch. Eight grouped cases cover all fields, both low-level paths, exact daemon payloads, errors, cancellation/reuse/channel loss and deadlines. Full suite, warning-free build, Ormolu/HLint, five audits, integrity checks and compiled README example pass. Full self-fess/abstraction review complete; no independent/live/upload/persistence/new-platform/package/commit claim. Evidence diagnostic-{source-probe,before,after,after-2,focused,final,example}.log under /var/folders/1q/mmxrlqw15hvd4gvfc86kjwhh0000gn/T/droid-hosted-resume.97zto82w. Generic call/standalone codecs remain caller-controlled rather than automatic runtime validators. Remaining mission/state/load/transport and final verification work stays open.
:SOURCE_REPO: droid-sdk-haskell
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/work/positron/droid-sdk-haskell
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

BugReportSourceSchema limits version/cliVersion/osVersion to 100 characters and platform/arch to 32. JSON Schema string lengths count Unicode code points; the published TypeScript/Zod string.max validators count UTF-16 code units, which differs for non-BMP characters. Source evidence: JSON Schema Draft-07 validation section 6.3.1; colinhacks/zod issue 3355; published SDK chunk-5UXINOXG.mjs BugReportSourceSchema.

The native canonical wire codec uses the supplied JSON Schema domain and BoundedText with code-point limits. Tests include exact-bound ASCII and emoji, empty strings, and over-limit rejection. Older-SDK interoperability is not established for the larger non-BMP inputs. Reconcile this in explicit protocol/compatibility handling before final parity sign-off; do not silently truncate values or claim the two contracts are identical.

Implemented renameDroidSession, changeDroidWorkingDirectory and getDroidRewindInfo in Factory.Droid, reusing typed Client operations. Add six typed low-level and connection-scoped operations: opened/available catalogs, session messages, cross-session search, archive/unarchive. Include archive-state notifications through existing dispatcher. Preserve numeric cursor units, defaults/bounds, null normalization, worktree fallback, force=false, extensions and all result/error variants. Native authenticated peers must prove no implicit session load, callback/lifecycle/cancellation and routing behavior. Source SDKs and live services remain untouched.

** Notes

Implemented six typed Client bindings and DaemonConnection operations for opened/available catalogs, stored messages, search, archive/unarchive, plus connection-wide archive-state observation. Reuses Core.connectionRequest, existing dispatcher/error subscriptions and message/host/tag/worktree/mission types. No implicit initialize/load, local-file operation, handle retirement, mutation retry, cache, second reader or lifecycle runtime. FC-09: droidProcess and prepareDroidProcess inherit the System.Process.proc close_fds=False default, exposing deliberately inheritable parent descriptors in ordinary high-level sessions. Make SDK-built Droid commands request close_fds=True through their existing constructor, while preserving explicitly supplied low-level CreateProcess behavior and intentional stdio/owned IPC channels. Add a harmless native descriptor-identity regression, explicit opt-out controls and macOS/Linux verification; never inspect unrelated descriptor contents. Keep original audit receipts. Local commits only; no push.

:SOURCE_REPO: droid-sdk-haskell
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/work/positron/droid-sdk-haskell
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Implemented typed RPC result extraction and generic scoped callback dispatch without changing wire schemas or choosing Haskell SDK attribution. Protocol.hs adds requestResult, decodeRpcResult and redacted RpcResultError: remote errors take precedence, missing/null/invalid results differ, and FromJSON decoding failure does not poison the channel. The exchange deadline does not claim to bound arbitrary pure decoder CPU time.

Factory.Droid.Protocol.Dispatch consumes the existing receiveRpcEvent intake, not a parallel transport reader. It provides onRpcEvent, onRpcNotification, onRpcError with terminal replay, and method-handler registration returning idempotent unsubscribe actions. Tokens prevent old unsubscribe/finalizer actions from deleting successors. Handlers retain in-flight snapshots and run in owned async workers; gated masked publication, active-ID deduplication and single-owner sealing cover lifetime races. Cleanup concurrently cancels and joins the loop/workers, including finalizers that wait for sibling cancellation. Response identity and caller-supplied envelope context are preserved; no incoming attribution is copied. Unregistered methods receive method-not-found; ordinary raw-handler exceptions produce a content-free internal error. These generic rules are not completed Droid permission defaults.

Verification: 2550 Haskell tests pass with -Werror (26 ProtocolSpec, 16 DispatchSpec and prior groups). All 55 process/protocol/dispatcher tests passed thirty consecutive runs with four test workers/two GHC capabilities. The native rpc peer exercises subscriptions, handler responses and correlated typed calls. In-memory peers verify replacement/unsubscribe, callback isolation, duplicate IDs, snapshots, reentrant requests, terminal replay, scope failure and handler resource cleanup. Read-only implementation review 522d82bc-927c-4cbd-ade0-7dd67935e56f found no issues; no code changes followed except formatting/test lint cleanup.

Ormolu, HLint, actionlint, schema audit and its 5 tests pass. Haddock documents Protocol and Dispatch at 100%, retaining existing dependency/Generic-link advisories. cabal check succeeds with the existing no-repository warning. README expressions run in isolated GHCi. Independent source distribution /tmp/droid-dispatch-final.CT42uQ/droid-sdk-0.1.0.0 builds warning-free and passes all Haskell/audit tests; build.log, haddock.log and repl.log are in its parent artifact directory.

Remaining: automatic request IDs, Droid-specific method bindings/default handlers and error policy, launcher, authentication/attribution, sessions/streams, daemon/REST and other goal capabilities. Generic dispatcher borrows its channel; cancelling a response write may poison that channel. Callbacks must support asynchronous cancellation and notification callbacks should remain brief. No live Factory call, credential helper, staging, commit, push or upstream-checkout edit occurred. Goal remains running; no checkpoint-review pause.

Optional/null distinctions, branch-specific extensions, generic nullable-ID responses and overlapping response alternatives remain schema-accurate. Metadata is data only: no Haskell-language extension, impersonation or automatic attribution-omission policy was selected.

src/Factory/Droid/Protocol.hs supplies withRpcChannel, requestReply, sendRpcMessage and receiveRpcEvent over caller-owned object transport actions. Atomic pending registration rejects active duplicate IDs; connection-unique IDs remain caller-supplied. Deadlines in microseconds cover writer acquisition, send and reply wait. Pending/late/duplicate replies, remote errors, malformed replies/envelopes, EOF, cancellation and scope cleanup are exercised. Interrupted writes poison the channel; response-wait cancellation removes only the request. Raw events have one consumer; the queue is unbounded. No automatic permission handler, callback registry, launcher, method-specific SDK exceptions or complete client API is claimed.

Tests added: 43 metadata, 81 envelope and 21 protocol checks. Existing process fixtures now validate complete envelopes; a native rpc peer interleaves notifications/server requests and reverses responses. All 2529 Haskell tests pass with -Werror. All 34 process/protocol tests passed thirty final consecutive runs with four workers and two GHC capabilities. Both review findings were reproduced before fixing: post-poison frames entered the event queue, and startup read failure was mislabeled as write failure. Dispatch now checks open state atomically; sender error sanitization preserves the recorded terminal cause through a nonblocking lookup. Final read-only review: no issues, OK (57a2b778-e236-4140-9aef-f9fb589922d1).

Ormolu, HLint, actionlint, the reference audit and its 5 regression tests pass. Haddock covers Metadata/RPC/Protocol at 100%; existing dependency/Generic-link and no-repository advisories remain. Extend the shared turn path with typed completion/usage/error results and explicit interruption that preserves settled session usability. Keep a success-checking convenience helper; separate true terminal failures from incomplete/protocol/callback failures. Correlate turn IDs where supplied, order interruption after submission, and prevent delayed interrupts from reaching a following turn. Use reference semantics and deterministic native tests, with no unauthorized live calls or unused codecs.

** Notes

Implemented sendDroidTurn returning every explicit terminal outcome with typed AgentTurnCompleted, reported usage/duration, ordered ErrorNotification data and partial text. sendPrompt/runDroid/streamDroid remain success-checking wrappers: completed/spec-handoff return, other settled reasons throw DroidTurnFailed carrying AgentTurnCompletionReason after restoring readiness. Incomplete/protocol/callback failures remain unavailable; tests for invalidated handles now trigger actual malformed events instead of a settled model error.

Fresh UUIDv4 message IDs from uuid-1.3.16.1 prevent reuse across resumed processes. Completion must carry the expected turnId, matching Python's correlated-turn contract; foreign completions are ignored and missing IDs fail. Reused existing completion/error codecs; no exhaustive-codec work added.

interruptDroidSession is idle no-op, registers a lifecycle lease for an active turn, waits for submission acknowledgement, and fences the next prompt until stop acknowledgement. Unknown sent outcomes use the established masked mutation invalidation; cancellation before sending releases the fence without invalidating. Deliberately removing either the submission or next-turn guard caused its native regression to fail; both guards restored.

All 62 high-level cases and 2746 tests pass; -Werror build/HLint/Ormolu pass; 30 parallel repetitions of 108 lifecycle/channel/dispatcher cases pass. Independent review found no issues. README terminal example compiles without execution. Haddock required dependency-doc downloads/builds and an intermediate timed-out pass; final offline retry completed with known link warnings. cabal check retains the existing repository advisory. Evidence: /tmp/droid-turn-outcomes.6buQQQ (verified-checks.log, stress-*.log, next-turn-mutant.log, submission-mutant.log, TerminalExample.hs, haddock-retry.log and exit file). No Factory/model call, commit or push. Rich message/event streaming and structured output remain separate functional work.

Inspected websockets 0.13.0.0: runClientWith allocates Socket and sets NoDelay outside its cleanup bracket; connect is the bracket acquisition, so exception/cancellation can bypass close. This SDK uses its own bracketOnError socket ownership and newClientConnection instead. connectionStrictUnicode is referenced only on server accept path; client JSON decoder must validate UTF-8. Record fixes/upstream issue consideration separately; do not post or patch external sources without authorization. No SDK correctness waiver: local wrapper/test protections required.

** Notes

SDK protections are now implemented and verified: the native transport owns sockets before NoDelay/connect, uses newClientConnection inside that scope, and validates incoming JSON/UTF-8 explicitly. Evidence: /tmp/droid-daemon-milestone.nd8zgR. The completed wrapper uses bracket ownership, superseding the earlier prototype's bracketOnError detail. No upstream source was modified and no issue was posted. Optional upstream report consideration remains deferred; it is not an outstanding SDK correctness waiver.

Implement the remaining typed session-settings snapshot capability without an optimistic cache. Read full settings from initialize/load replies and account for before/after reply notification ordering, explicit spec resets, replacement/rollback epochs and absent fields in partial settings_updated payloads. Current request replies bypass dispatcher intake, so a prefix drain alone does not establish snapshot-versus-update order. Resolve this at the existing protocol/observer seam before promising a current view; do not silently synthesize peer state from acknowledgements or drop raced updates. Baseline Python _convert.py:364-437 and session.py metadata binding, and selected CLI O8H/wet contracts. Unused codec coverage is not a prerequisite.

** Notes

Implemented getDroidSettings and full SessionSettings decoding on the existing connection. requestReplyObserved preserves immediate RPC correlation while atomically queuing only the first accepted reply observation at its receive position; dequeue and STM observation commit together. One connection reducer consumes notifications before user observers. No new reader, worker, dependency or epoch framework. Getter checks lifecycle and stored session ID atomically, never sends or waits, and exposes only last dispatcher-observed state. Source/replacement/rollback ownership is unchanged. The final fold is strict to avoid accumulating deferred update chains.

Selected CLI O8H at byte 204404190 and emitter at 218901203 establish the full snapshot and mixed patch/snapshot contract. Omitted spec model, spec reasoning and mission settings clear current observations; other omitted fields retain prior reports. Mission objects replace wholly. Empty lists, empty spec text and False are preserved; malformed observations make the view invalid until a valid full load, without invalidating an otherwise idle handle. Invalid enum fallback supplies no new observation. The selected notifier does not emit tags. Untagged and same-ID cross-epoch events remain inherently ambiguous; no latest-peer-state or ACK-derived truth is promised. README and parity/development notes document these limits and the older Python null-clearing difference.

Verification: all 3005 Haskell tests, 173 high-level cases, 30x227 parallel channel/dispatcher/high-level checks, warning-free builds, HLint, Ormolu, five Python audits, attachment FD/race probes, actionlint, compiled getter/subscription example and Haddock. Removing spec/mission clearing and first-response admission independently failed their regressions; both mutations were restored. Independent read-only review found no issues and judged architecture aligned. Accepted-reply cancellation followed by rollback has component/source evidence rather than a dedicated end-to-end settings assertion.

Source distribution was independently built and tested in a separate unpacked tree; the final strictness change was reapplied there and recompiled. Final archive, unpacked source and worktree match all 95 packaged-file hashes. Independent 3005 tests, schema/audit tests, FD/race probes and example compilation passed. Two compound verification invocations exhausted 300-second tool deadlines while builds were in progress; no compiler errors or surviving build processes were observed, and unchanged checks completed on continuation. Existing Haddock dependency/Generic link warnings and cabal no-repository advisory remain. Evidence: /tmp/droid-observed-settings.pfpTXj, especially strict-final.log, strict-stress-*.log, distribution-strict-resumed.log, verified-package-files-final.json, source-final-sha256.txt, haddock-final.log and mutation logs. RFC3339 §5.7 distinguishes actual occurrence/announcements from grammar and warns against generating unannounced leap events. :EXTERNAL_REF: pi-goal:mtnsrj8c-uuc099/required-leap-second-contract
:SOURCE_REPO: droid-sdk-haskell
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/work/positron/droid-sdk-haskell
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Rfc3339Timestamp validates RFC 3339 lexical syntax, Gregorian dates, numeric offsets, arbitrary fractional precision and the UTC month-end position of a positive leap second. It does not consult IERS announcements, validate actual leap-second occurrence dates or account for any future negative leap-second announcement. RFC 3339 section 5.7 distinguishes these occurrence rules from grammar. Before claiming complete date-time format conformance or final SDK parity, establish and test the required occurrence-validation policy against authoritative evidence without normalizing wire spelling or inventing future leap events. Current terminal wire types and tests cover the explicitly documented grammar/calendar boundary only.

** Acceptance Criteria

Obr: hsdk-w8y. Obr parent protocol-baseline-decisions. Determine required-operation impact; resolve/test exposed behavior or document nonblocking scope with evidence. Preserve codecs/tests; no exhaustive date-time audit.

Completion checklist:

- Implement demonstrated gaps through existing abstractions, preserving exact values, permissions, ownership and async-exception identity.
- After SDK resumption execute focused applicable normal/variant/malformed/error/deadline/cancellation/cleanup regression checks. Use native peers and preserve existing tests; broad checks belong at coherent boundaries.
- Update necessary API docs/examples and parity evidence, compile relevant examples and run full fess before closure; distinguish self-review, independent review and execution.
- No SDK edits/tests before explicit /goal-resume. No live calls, outreach, account changes, commits or publication without separate authorization.

:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       hsdk-zti
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: feature

:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-09-06 Sun 19:23]
:CLOSE_REASON: Local MVP delivered: live stream/result/cleanup verified within two authorized attempts; native follow-up/lifecycle coverage and final package/example checks pass. Evidence: /tmp/droid-local-final.z239ap7y
:SOURCE_REPO: droid-sdk-haskell
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/work/positron/droid-sdk-haskell
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

Delivered the narrowed local Droid workflow: prompts, streamed text, final results, same-session follow-up and scoped cleanup. Exhaustive SDK/schema parity and daemon/WebSocket work remain backlog, not delivery gates.

Runtime baseline: installed CLI 0.212.1 / protocol 1.201.1. Launch arguments are exec --input-format stream-jsonrpc --output-format stream-jsonrpc. Minimal initialization uses machineId=default, absolute cwd, optional modelId and autoRejectPermissionRequests=true. Existing CLI authentication is inherited, unsupported SDK identity is omitted, and inherited FACTORY_UPSTREAM_CLIENT_TYPE/FACTORY_UPSTREAM_SDK labels are removed rather than impersonating Python or TypeScript. No credential discovery/helper implementation was added.

Factory.Droid exposes DroidOptions/defaultDroidOptions, withDroidSession, sendPrompt, runDroid, streamDroid, session ID and final text result. Ordinary callers do not manage envelopes, IDs or threads. Existing process/RPC/dispatcher code owns the lifecycle. Text deltas and prefilled messages accumulate until explicit completion; irrelevant and explicitly foreign-session events are ignored. Failed/cancelled turns invalidate the session and request interruption. Permission and question requests default to cancellation. examples/Main.hs demonstrates two prompts in one scope with a two-minute per-turn timeout.

The first authorized real-CLI attempt failed with DroidInvalidEvent and still reaped the child. It exposed an incorrectly required sessionId in local notification parameters; the installed protocol and reference schema make that field optional. The parser now accepts untagged local notifications while preserving explicit session filtering, with a native regression test.

The second authorized prompt attempt used a compiled Haskell check in a fresh temporary directory. It streamed HELLO at 4.476 seconds, asserted matching final text and session ID, and completed with exit code zero in 6.771 seconds. The monitored child was gone after scope exit. Two prompt attempts were used in total; no additional model request was made. Same-session follow-up is verified offline, not by a completed live two-turn run.

Final verification on Apple Silicon macOS/GHC 9.10.3: build all with -Werror; 2691 Haskell tests including 11 high-level native-peer checks; five reference-audit tests; HLint, Ormolu and actionlint; Haddock and cabal check. One freshly unpacked source distribution built the library, tests and example, passed the same tests/audit, and matched all 82 packaged source files. Its compiled two-turn example passed against a native offline peer; README examples passed ghc -fno-code -Wall -Werror. Existing Haddock dependency/Generic link warnings and the no-repository Cabal advisory remain documented, not suppressed.

Evidence: /tmp/droid-local-final.z239ap7y contains live-once.hs, live-second-evidence.json, live output logs, focused-tests.log, final-checks.log, haddock.log, distribution.log, offline-example output, readme-check.log, verified-package-files.json and the reviewed mvp-diff.patch. A transient macOS loader stall delayed intermediate tool invocations; final checks passed with the same pinned native tools, without a source workaround or disabled check.

The unfinished WebSocket source/test remain preserved at /tmp/droid-websocket-deferred.nr56Lv and excluded from the package. Remaining parity and broader platform/compiler work are backlog.
